00051: 
00052: def positive(v):
00053:     assert math.isfinite(v) and v > 0
00054:     return v
00055: 
00056: 
00057: def point(p):
00058:     assert len(p) == 3 and all(math.isfinite(v) for v in p)
00059:     return tuple(p)
00060: 
00061: 
00062: def attach(parent, key, node):
00063:     assert key not in parent.members, key
00064:     return parent.include(key, node)
00065: 
00066: 
00067: def cube(parent, key, size, center, color, radius=None):
00068:     size = tuple(positive(v) for v in size)
00069:     center = point(center)
00070:     if radius is None:
00071:         node = box(size, center=center, color=color)
00072:     else:
00073:         positive(radius)
00074:         assert radius <= min(size) / 2, (key, size, radius)
00075:         node = fillet_box(size, radius, center=center, color=color)
00076:     return attach(parent, key, node)
00077: 
00078: 
00079: def tube(parent, key, points, radius, color):
00080:     positive(radius)
00081:     points = [point(p) for p in points]
00082:     assert len(points) >= 2
00083:     assert all(sum((a[k] - b[k]) ** 2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-16
00084:                for a, b in zip(points, points[1:])), key
00085:     return attach(parent, key,
00086:                   sweep_tube(points=points, radius=radius, color=color))
00087: 
00088: 
00089: def cylinder(parent, key, radius, height, center, color):
00090:     positive(radius)
00091:     positive(height)
00092:     return attach(parent, key, rod(radius, length=height,
00093:                                   center=point(center), color=color))
00094: 
00095: 
00096: def segment(parent, key, radius, start, end, color):
00097:     positive(radius)
00098:     start, end = point(start), point(end)
00099:     assert sum((start[k] - end[k]) ** 2 for k in range(3)) > 1e-16
00100:     return attach(parent, key, rod(radius, start=start, end=end, color=color))
00101: 
00102: 
00103: def sphere(parent, key, radius, center, color):
00104:     positive(radius)
00105:     return attach(parent, key, ball(radius, center=point(center), color=color))

00316: def meshes_under(label):
00317:     parent = bpy.data.objects.get(label)
00318:     assert parent is not None, label
00319:     result = []
00320: 
00321:     def walk(obj):
00322:         if obj.type == 'MESH':
00323:             result.append(obj)
00324:         for child in obj.children:
00325:             walk(child)
00326: 
00327:     walk(parent)
00328:     assert result, label
00329:     return result
00330: 
00331: 
00332: def isolated_materials(obj):
00333:     result = []
00334:     for slot in obj.material_slots:
00335:         if slot.material is not None:
00336:             mat = slot.material.copy()
00337:             slot.material = mat
00338:             mat.use_nodes = True
00339:             result.append(mat)
00340:     return result
00341: 
00342: 
00343: for obj in meshes_under('architecture'):
00344:     if obj.dimensions.z < .20:
00345:         for mat in isolated_materials(obj):
00346:             nodes, links = mat.node_tree.nodes, mat.node_tree.links
00347:             bs = nodes.get('Principled BSDF')
00348:             assert bs is not None
00349:             tex = nodes.new('ShaderNodeTexNoise')
00350:             tex.inputs['Scale'].default_value = 155
00351:             tex.inputs['Detail'].default_value = 3
00352:             ramp = nodes.new('ShaderNodeValToRGB')
00353:             ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].position = .25
00354:             ramp.color_ramp.elements[0].color = (.065, .060, .044, 1)
00355:             ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].position = .75
00356:             ramp.color_ramp.elements[1].color = (.49, .44, .33, 1)
00357:             links.new(tex.outputs['Fac'], ramp.inputs['Fac'])

00055: def cube(parent, key, size, center, color):
00056:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) and v > 0 for v in size)
00057:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in center)
00058:     assert key not in parent.members
00059:     return parent.include(key, box(size=size, center=center, color=color))
00060: 
00061: def add_rod(parent, key, radius, start, end, color):
00062:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00063:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in (*start,*end))
00064:     assert sum((a-b)**2 for a,b in zip(start,end)) > 1e-16
00065:     assert key not in parent.members
00066:     return parent.include(key, rod(radius=radius,start=start,end=end,color=color))
00067: 
00068: def add_tube(parent, key, points, radius, color):
00069:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00070:     assert len(points) >= 2
00071:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for p in points for v in p)
00072:     assert all(sum((a-b)**2 for a,b in zip(p,q)) > 1e-16
00073:                for p,q in zip(points[:-1],points[1:]))
00074:     assert key not in parent.members
00075:     return parent.include(key,sweep_tube(points=points,radius=radius,color=color))

00053: # Validated construction helpers. No fillet_box is used in this program.
00054: def finite_vector(values):
00055:     return all(math.isfinite(float(v)) for v in values)
00056: 
00057: def cube(parent, key, size, center, color):
00058:     assert finite_vector(size) and all(v > 0 for v in size)
00059:     assert finite_vector(center)
00060:     assert key not in parent.members, "Duplicate include key: " + key
00061:     return parent.include(
00062:         key, box(size=size, center=center, color=color)
00063:     )
00064: 
00065: def add_rod(parent, key, radius, start, end, color):
00066:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00067:     assert finite_vector(start) and finite_vector(end)
00068:     assert sum((a - b) ** 2 for a, b in zip(start, end)) > 1e-16
00069:     assert key not in parent.members
00070:     return parent.include(
00071:         key, rod(radius=radius, start=start, end=end, color=color)
00072:     )
00073: 
00074: def add_tube(parent, key, points, radius, color):
00075:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00076:     assert len(points) >= 2
00077:     assert all(finite_vector(point) for point in points)
00078:     assert all(
00079:         sum((a - b) ** 2 for a, b in zip(p, q)) > 1e-16
00080:         for p, q in zip(points[:-1], points[1:])
00081:     )
00082:     assert key not in parent.members
00083:     return parent.include(
00084:         key, sweep_tube(points=points, radius=radius, color=color)
00085:     )

00347:     meshes = []

00350:             meshes.append(child)

00005: # Visual estimates: timber x=.17-.30; hood x=.42-.73, y=.045-.43;
00006: # island top x=.33-.90, y=.55-.63; island contacts y=.72-.85;
00007: # rear recess x=.30-.59, y=.49-.56; ovens x=.67-.79, y=.43-.58.
00008: # Observed in step 1: kitchen is too small, timber is too central,
00009: # and foreground wall/ceiling surfaces obscure the intended composition.
00010: # Hypothesis: distant camera outside the architectural shell causes the
00011: # global framing disagreement. Viewing azimuth also understates island depth.
00012: # Correction: move camera closer and inside the shell, adjust its azimuth,
00013: # relocate the foreground timber to retain the reference overlap, and extend
00014: # the side shell outward. Object dimensions and finishes otherwise persist.
00015: # Inventory remains: tiled floor, exposed beams, two timber supports, cabinets,
00016: # recess, ovens, island, sink/faucet, hob, extractor and red kettle.
00017: # Scale and camera calibration remain visual estimates.

00054: def cube(parent, key, size, center, color):
00055:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) and v > 0 for v in size)
00056:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in center)
00057:     assert key not in parent.members
00058:     return parent.include(key, box(size=size, center=center, color=color))
00059: 
00060: def add_rod(parent, key, radius, start, end, color):
00061:     assert radius > 0 and math.isfinite(radius)
00062:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in (*start, *end))
00063:     assert sum((a-b)**2 for a,b in zip(start,end)) > 1e-16
00064:     assert key not in parent.members
00065:     return parent.include(key, rod(radius=radius, start=start,
00066:                                    end=end, color=color))
00067: 
00068: def add_tube(parent, key, points, radius, color):
00069:     assert radius > 0 and math.isfinite(radius)
00070:     assert len(points) >= 2
00071:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for p in points for v in p)
00072:     assert all(sum((a-b)**2 for a,b in zip(p,q)) > 1e-16
00073:                for p,q in zip(points[:-1],points[1:]))
00074:     assert key not in parent.members
00075:     return parent.include(key, sweep_tube(points=points, radius=radius,
00076:                                           color=color))

00055: def cube(parent, key, size, center, color):
00056:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) and v > 0 for v in size)
00057:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in center)
00058:     assert key not in parent.members
00059:     return parent.include(key, box(size=size, center=center, color=color))
00060: 
00061: def add_rod(parent, key, radius, start, end, color):
00062:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00063:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in (*start,*end))
00064:     assert sum((a-b)**2 for a,b in zip(start,end)) > 1e-16
00065:     assert key not in parent.members
00066:     return parent.include(key, rod(radius=radius,start=start,end=end,color=color))
00067: 
00068: def add_tube(parent, key, points, radius, color):
00069:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00070:     assert len(points) >= 2
00071:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for p in points for v in p)
00072:     assert all(sum((a-b)**2 for a,b in zip(p,q)) > 1e-16
00073:                for p,q in zip(points[:-1],points[1:]))
00074:     assert key not in parent.members
00075:     return parent.include(key,sweep_tube(points=points,radius=radius,color=color))

00055: def put(parent, key, node):
00056:     assert key not in parent.members, key
00057:     return parent.include(key, node)
00058: 
00059: def block(parent, key, size, center, color):
00060:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) and v > 0 for v in size)
00061:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in center)
00062:     return put(parent, key, box(size=size, center=center, color=color))
00063: 
00064: def shaft(parent, key, p, q, radius=.025, color=IRON):
00065:     assert math.isfinite(radius) and radius > 0
00066:     assert all(math.isfinite(v) for v in (*p, *q))
00067:     assert sum((a-b)**2 for a, b in zip(p, q)) > 1e-12
00068:     return put(parent, key, rod(radius=radius, start=p, end=q, color=color))
00069: 
00070: def hoop(parent, key, center, radius, thickness, color=IRON, plane="xz"):
00071:     assert radius > 0 and thickness > 0
00072:     x, y, z = center
00073:     pts = []
00074:     for i in range(21):
00075:         a = 2*math.pi*i/20
00076:         if plane == "xz":
00077:             pts.append((x+radius*math.cos(a), y, z+radius*math.sin(a)))
00078:         elif plane == "yz":
00079:             pts.append((x, y+radius*math.cos(a), z+radius*math.sin(a)))
00080:         else:
00081:             pts.append((x+radius*math.cos(a), y+radius*math.sin(a), z))
00082:     assert all(sum((a-b)**2 for a, b in zip(p, q)) > 1e-12
00083:                for p, q in zip(pts, pts[1:]))
00084:     return put(parent, key, sweep_tube(points=pts, radius=thickness, color=color))
